消息 17 assistant

外部DeepSeek原记录 thinking
The s0 task language was "Pick the akita black bowl on the cookie box and place it on the plate". My seed 1's language: "pick up the black bowl on the ramekin and place it on the plate". So the relation is inverted: target = bowl ON THE RAMEKIN.

Looking at the image: the bowl at right (col ~730, row ~450) sits on a small grey fluted cup = glazed_rim_porcelain_ramekin. The bowl at bottom-center sits on a red/yellow cookie box.

So the target is the bowl at high-res pixel ~(730, 450) → world (-0.2544, 0.1976, 0.957). Wait that x=-0.2544. Hmm, that seems far. And plate at (0.0171, 0.1962).
